#################### 37_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_V1.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.7

37 — SECURITY THREAT MODEL v1
=============================

METHOD
STRIDE-inspired analysis over trust boundaries.

TRUST BOUNDARIES

1 Local UI <-> Local Domain
2 Domain <-> Native Audio/MIDI/Plugin processes
3 Client <-> Backend
4 Client <-> Storage Providers
5 Backend <-> Storage Providers
6 Client <-> Third-party Plugins
7 Client <-> AI Models/Providers
8 Public Community <-> Private Projects
9 Admin <-> Production Data

ASSETS TO PROTECT
private recordings
videos
project metadata
credentials/tokens
rights/split records
payment data later
private messages
plugin/provider secrets
release approvals
user identity
local files.

THREATS

T1 Spoofed user/session
Mitigation:
short-lived tokens, secure refresh, MFA/passkeys later, server authorization.

T2 IDOR / project access
Mitigation:
authorize every project/asset operation server-side; never trust client projectId.

T3 Malicious storage connector
Mitigation:
connector permissions, domain allowlist, review, isolated credentials, explicit install.

T4 SSRF through custom provider URL
Mitigation:
backend URL validation, private-network deny policy, DNS rebinding protection.

T5 OAuth token leakage
Mitigation:
secure store, never project JSON/logs, minimum scopes, revocation.

T6 Malicious plugin
Mitigation:
separate process, scan/quarantine, restricted filesystem where practical, crash isolation.

T7 Malicious model/repository code
Mitigation:
no auto trust_remote_code, model hash, sandbox runtime, review licence/code.

T8 Project file attack
Mitigation:
schema validation, size/depth limits, no arbitrary executable content, zip-slip protection.

T9 Path traversal on archive extract
Mitigation:
canonicalize paths, reject .. / absolute paths / symlink escapes.

T10 Huge/decompression bomb
Mitigation:
input size, ratio, duration, frame limits, streaming decode.

T11 Media decoder vulnerability
Mitigation:
maintained codecs, sandbox workers, patch cadence, fuzzing.

T12 CRDT/update denial of service
Mitigation:
update size/rate limits, compaction, authenticated peers, document partition.

T13 Collaboration privilege escalation
Mitigation:
server membership/permission check for relay; rights approvals outside casual CRDT.

T14 Public/private data leak
Mitigation:
separate stores/indexes, signed short-lived media URLs, permission-filtered search.

T15 XSS community content
Mitigation:
escape/sanitize, CSP, no raw HTML by default.

T16 CSRF
Mitigation:
same-site secure cookie strategy or token pattern; CSRF token where required.

T17 Supply-chain dependency compromise
Mitigation:
lockfiles, SBOM, signatures/checksums, dependency review, secret scan.

T18 Update compromise
Mitigation:
signed native updates, HTTPS, channel validation, rollback.

T19 Firebase/Supabase security-rule mistake
Mitigation:
provider adapter integration tests, emulator tests, deny-by-default.

T20 Firestore cost abuse
Mitigation:
rate limits, App Check where useful, local throttling, cost alerts; local app unaffected.

T21 Storage deletion mistake
Mitigation:
distinguish Delete Replica vs Delete Asset; confirmation; retention grace.

T22 Ransomware/local corruption
Mitigation:
snapshots, content hashes, optional independent backup replica.

T23 Plugin state secret leakage
Mitigation:
do not assume plugin state is safe to publish; project visibility controls.

T24 AI data exfiltration
Mitigation:
show provider/inputs, opt-in cloud AI, local model option, data-classification policy.

T25 Prompt injection from imported docs/community
Mitigation:
AI tool permissions; retrieved text is data, not authority; confirmation for actions.

T26 Admin abuse
Mitigation:
least privilege, MFA, audit, break-glass process, no broad content access by default.

T27 Rights approval tampering
Mitigation:
append-only approvals/audit, explicit amendment workflow.

T28 Replay webhook
Mitigation:
signature + event-id dedupe + timestamp window.

T29 DVS/hardware raw message abuse
Mitigation:
SysEx/device-writing permission and allowlisted profile capabilities.

T30 Realtime audio safety
Mitigation:
NaN/Inf guard, feedback detection, limiter/panic, bounded parameter values.

SECURITY REQUIREMENTS
SEC-001 local-only works without network identity.
SEC-002 all provider tokens absent from project archive.
SEC-003 archive extraction traversal tests.
SEC-004 private project denied to unrelated user.
SEC-005 plugin crash isolated.
SEC-006 malicious extension denied undeclared permission.
SEC-007 AI cannot upload project without explicit provider policy/permission.
SEC-008 CRDT oversized update rejected.
SEC-009 rights approval audited.
SEC-010 public search excludes private assets.
SEC-011 update signatures verified.
SEC-012 logs redact tokens.
SEC-013 delete replica does not delete canonical asset silently.
SEC-014 custom storage endpoint SSRF-tested.
SEC-015 threat model reviewed at each major trust-boundary change.

39 — FIRST VERTICAL SLICE IMPLEMENTATION PLAN
=============================================

OUTCOME
A real Poietek Web/PWA project can:

11 Reopen same project offline.

NO Firebase account required.

#################### 40_BUSINESS_HOSTING_COST_SUSTAINABILITY_MODEL.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.7

40 — BUSINESS, HOSTING COST & SUSTAINABILITY MODEL
==================================================

PRINCIPLE
Poietek's core creative product should not require Poietek to finance every
creator's terabytes of audio/video.

Architecture:
Local First
+ Bring Your Own Cloud
+ Optional Poietek-managed services.

CURRENT PUBLIC PRICING SNAPSHOT
Checked 11 August 2026. Pricing changes; never hard-code these values in product
logic.

FIREBASE SPARK
Official Firebase material currently lists:
- Firestore: 1 GiB stored no-cost, 20,000 document writes/day, 50,000 reads/day,
  20,000 deletes/day.
- Firebase Hosting: 10 GB hosting storage no-cost and 10 GB/month transfer no-cost.
- Cloud Storage for Firebase now requires Blaze billing plan access; the no-cost
  Spark plan cannot access Cloud Storage buckets as of the February 3, 2026
  requirement.
- Firebase Authentication Spark limits must be checked before launch; current
  auth documentation describes 3,000 DAU for most sign-in providers on Spark.

Sources:
https://firebase.google.com/pricing
https://firebase.google.com/docs/hosting/usage-quotas-pricing
https://firebase.google.com/docs/storage/faqs-storage-changes-announced-sept-2024
https://firebase.google.com/docs/auth

SUPABASE FREE
Current public pricing lists:
- 500 MB database per project.
- 50,000 monthly active users.
- 1 GB file storage.
- 5 GB egress and 5 GB cached egress.
- 200 peak realtime connections and 2 million messages/month.
- free projects may pause after one week of inactivity.
Source:
https://supabase.com/pricing

CLOUDFLARE R2
Current official pricing:
- free tier: 10 GB-month Standard storage/month,
- 1 million Class A operations/month,
- 10 million Class B operations/month,
- Internet egress free.
- Standard storage above free tier: $0.015/GB-month.
Source:
https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/pricing/

BACKBLAZE B2
Current official marketing pricing begins around $6.95/TB/month for standard B2,
with its published egress/transaction terms.
Source:
https://www.backblaze.com/cloud-storage/pricing

PROTOTYPE COST MODEL

Goal: £/$0 platform spend where possible.

Client:
local project/media.

Web hosting:
Firebase Hosting Spark.

Auth/project directory:
Firebase Spark.

Large media:
local only / user BYOC.

Git:
GitHub free repo where appropriate.

Models:
local cache / free public model sources subject to licence.

RESULT
The prototype can test product flow without offering managed bulk cloud storage.

ALPHA COST MODEL

Still local/BYOC first.

Add optional managed object storage for:
thumbnails
small previews
collaboration assets
users who explicitly choose Poietek cloud.

Use spend caps/budget alerts.

BETA / PUBLIC

Separate cost centers:

CONTROL PLANE
auth
DB
realtime
notifications.

MEDIA PLANE
object storage
egress
transcode.

COMPUTE PLANE
AI
stem separation
video render.

COMMUNITY PLANE
feed/search/moderation.

Do not bundle all cost into one opaque subscription.

RECOMMENDED MONETIZATION SHAPE

CORE
Free/community or fair one-time purchase remains viable if sustainable.

OPTIONAL RECURRING
Poietek Cloud:
managed backup
storage
high-speed team sync
cloud AI
render/transcode
large collaboration.

BYOC USERS
Can use Google Drive/OneDrive/TeraBox/WebDAV/S3/etc. subject to provider policies.
Poietek may charge nothing for raw storage it does not provide.

MARKETPLACE
Optional revenue share from third-party plugins/presets/templates/services.

CREATOR MONETIZATION
Transaction fee only when actual commerce occurs.

COST SAFETY

1 Never stream waveform meters through paid DB writes.
2 Never put PCM/video in Firestore.
3 Deduplicate by content hash.
4 Upload once; use replicas.
5 Proxy video.
6 Cache thumbnails.
7 Local AI when practical.
8 Queue background work.
9 Put maximum cost per job.
10 Explicit cloud-compute confirmation for expensive jobs.

UNIT ECONOMICS METRICS

Control plane cost / MAU
Storage GB / active creator
Egress GB / active creator
AI cost / active creator
Transcode minutes / active creator
Support cost / active creator
Marketplace GMV
Cloud conversion rate.

SCENARIO FORMULA

MonthlyManagedMediaCost =
storage_gb * storage_rate
+ billable_write_ops
+ billable_read_ops
+ egress
+ transcode
+ AI.

BYOC reduces storage_gb and egress borne by Poietek.

FREE TIER ABUSE
Need:
rate limits
verified email/account where needed
App Check/anti-abuse
job quotas
no anonymous unlimited AI/render.

NO "UNLIMITED" PROMISE
Do not market unlimited cloud storage/AI without an enforceable economic model.

BUSINESS ACCEPTANCE
BUS-001 local-only user creates without paid infrastructure.
BUS-002 provider outage does not force Poietek media egress.
BUS-003 cost dashboard separates media/control/compute.
BUS-004 per-job AI/render cost estimated before expensive run.
BUS-005 managed storage optional.
BUS-006 BYOC user can export/migrate.
BUS-007 quotas fail gracefully.
BUS-008 billing does not affect ownership of local project.
BUS-009 subscription cancellation allows data export.
BUS-010 pricing assumptions reviewed quarterly before public scale.

#################### 41_COMMAND_BUS_DOMAIN_EVENTS_UNDO_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.8

41 — COMMAND BUS, DOMAIN EVENTS & UNDO CONTRACT v1
==================================================

COMPANION FILES
command.schema.v1.json
event.schema.v1.json

PURPOSE
Every meaningful project mutation must travel through one command pathway so UI,
MIDI hardware, keyboard shortcuts, automation and AI do not create competing
state-change systems.

COMMAND LIFECYCLE

Intent
-> Command
-> Validate
-> Authorize
-> Execute
-> Persist local transaction
-> Emit domain events
-> Record undo inverse / snapshot
-> Queue sync
-> Update read models

EXAMPLE COMMANDS

Project:
CreateProject
RenameProject
SetTempo
SetTimeSignature

Tracks:
AddTrack
DeleteTrack
RenameTrack
MoveTrack

Clips:
AddAudioClip
MoveClip
TrimClip
SplitClip
SetClipGain
SetClipFade
DeleteClip

MIDI:
AddMidiNote
MoveMidiNote
ResizeMidiNote
SetMidiVelocity
SetMidiExpression

Sampler:
AssignAssetToPad
SetPadChokeGroup
CreateSlices

Mixer:
SetTrackGain
SetTrackPan
SetMute
SetSolo
InsertPlugin
RemovePlugin
SetSendLevel

Storage:
SetStoragePolicy
AddAssetReplica
RemoveAssetReplica

Collaboration:
AddComment
ResolveComment
CreateSnapshot

Rights:
AssignContributor
ProposeSplit
ApproveSplit
RejectSplit

UNDO RULES

A command must declare one of:
REVERSIBLE
SNAPSHOT_REQUIRED
NON_UNDOABLE_AUDITED

Examples:
MoveClip -> reversible with inverse.
ImportAsset -> project reference reversible; physical asset garbage collection deferred.
ApproveSplit -> audited amendment workflow, not casual Ctrl+Z.

CommandResult {
  revision
  events[]
  warnings[]
  changedObjectIds[]
}

CONCURRENCY

expectedRevision may be supplied for commands requiring optimistic concurrency.
CRDT-backed commands may omit global expectedRevision if operation merge semantics
are safe.

AI

AI never edits objects directly.
It produces ProposedCommand[].
User preview/accept invokes normal CommandBus.

HARDWARE

MIDI mapping resolves physical event -> logical target -> command or realtime
parameter control.

Realtime ephemeral controls such as fader preview may update engine state at high
frequency but commit canonical automation/parameter state through commands.

EVENTS

Domain events are facts:
TrackAdded
ClipMoved
AssetImported
ContributorAssigned
StorageReplicaAvailable
SplitProposalCreated

Events are versioned independently from command versions.

SYNC

Not every domain event must be sent over the network exactly as emitted.
Sync adapter converts canonical mutations to collaborative operations.

COMMAND ACCEPTANCE
CMD-001 UI cannot mutate canonical track state directly.
CMD-002 same SetTrackGain command works from UI and mapped hardware.
CMD-003 inverse restores SplitClip transaction where supported.
CMD-004 audited rights approval cannot disappear via ordinary undo.
CMD-005 duplicate commandId is idempotent where retried.
CMD-006 rejected validation changes no state.
CMD-007 failed persistence changes no visible committed state.
CMD-008 event envelope validates.
CMD-009 AI accepted proposal uses same command path.
CMD-010 migrations may issue system commands but record migration context.

#################### 42_STORAGE_PROVIDER_SDK_ROUTING_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.8

42 — STORAGE PROVIDER SDK & ROUTING CONTRACT v1
===============================================

COMPANION
StorageProvider.ts
storage-provider-manifest.schema.v1.json

ROUTING RULE
Project references canonical Asset IDs. StorageRouter decides which provider
replica satisfies a read/write policy.

StoragePolicy example:

Working:
local-fast

Backup:
any connected provider with resumableUpload and sufficient quota

Video archive:
prefer object_storage or user-selected bulk provider

Git mirror:
git_forge only, metadata/text whitelist

AUTO RECOMMENDATION ALGORITHM

Score candidate:
+ local availability
+ free capacity
+ resumable upload
+ range read for media
+ team ACL if team project
+ user preference
- provider disconnected
- provider quota low
- unsupported file size
- git forge for large raw media
- provider policy forbids operation

Recommendation never performs first remote upload without consent.

INSTALL / CONNECT

Connector state:
NOT_INSTALLED
INSTALLED_NOT_CONNECTED
CONNECTED
REAUTH_REQUIRED
ERROR
DISABLED

The UI may offer installation. Skip always works.

DELETE SEMANTICS

RemoveReplica(assetId, replicaId)
!=
DeleteCanonicalAsset(assetId)

Canonical delete requires reference analysis and retention policy.

PROVIDER CONTRACT TEST SUITE
- connect/disconnect
- quota
- upload/download checksum
- interrupted upload
- range read if declared
- remove
- permission denial
- invalid credential
- quota exceeded
- offline
- path/name encoding
- large-file boundary

SECURITY
Connectors receive only declared capabilities.
OAuth tokens are outside project state.
Custom endpoint connectors must pass SSRF protections where backend-mediated.

ACCEPTANCE
STSDK-001 Local provider always available.
STSDK-002 connector can be absent without project failure.
STSDK-003 capability declarations match tests.
STSDK-004 auto router rejects git forge for huge WAV by default.
STSDK-005 user override remains possible when technically legal/supported.
STSDK-006 checksum verifies after upload.
STSDK-007 credentials never serialized.
STSDK-008 failed backup leaves local asset intact.

#################### 43_DEVICE_PROFILE_HARDWARE_LEARN_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.8

43 — DEVICE PROFILE SCHEMA & HARDWARE LEARN CONTRACT v1
=======================================================

COMPANION
device-profile.schema.v1.json

PROFILE PRINCIPLE
Device profiles describe physical capabilities and mappings. They do not own
project musical meaning.

LEARN WIZARD

1 Detect ports.
2 Capture identity if available.
3 Ask user to press representative pads/keys.
4 Turn knobs/encoders.
5 Move faders.
6 Press transport.
7 Detect message pattern.
8 Name/classify control.
9 Test feedback if explicitly permitted.
10 Save profile.
11 Run validation suite.
12 Optional publish.

RAW INPUT EXAMPLES

MIDI Note:
{ protocol:"midi1", kind:"note", channel:10, note:36 }

CC:
{ protocol:"midi1", kind:"cc", channel:1, cc:74 }

Pitch:
{ protocol:"midi1", kind:"pitch_bend", channel:1 }

HID:
adapter-specific normalized descriptor.

OUTPUT FEEDBACK
Must be explicit and permissioned.
Examples:
LED
RGB
motor fader
screen text
ring indicator.

Do not send undocumented SysEx merely because a profile claims it.

PROFILE DISTRIBUTION

Builtin
Community
User-local
Manufacturer

Verification label must match evidence.

SCHEMA ACCEPTANCE
DEV-001 Generic MIDI keyboard validates.
DEV-002 16-pad profile validates.
DEV-003 unknown extra extension fields retained under extensions.
DEV-004 profile with invalid control type rejected.
DEV-005 manufacturer_verified unavailable without trusted verification metadata.
DEV-006 profile never contains OAuth/storage credentials.

PERMISSION MODEL

Default: no permission.

Examples:
A theme should need none.
A storage connector may need network + storage.
A workflow should not receive microphone automatically.
An AI provider may need project.read only when user invokes it.

INSTALL UX

Show:
publisher
license
version
platforms
requested permissions
network domains
verification
size.

[Install]
[Cancel]

Permission increase on update requires confirmation.

RUNTIME
Web-safe modules prefer WASM/sandboxed execution.
Native modules require signing/review strategy.
Themes cannot run arbitrary code.

MARKETPLACE
Manifest is the technical declaration, not proof of safety.

ACCEPTANCE

#################### 45_FIREBASE_WEB_MVP_DATA_MAPPING_SECURITY.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.8

45 — FIREBASE WEB MVP DATA MAPPING & SECURITY RULES
===================================================

COMPANION
firestore.rules
firestore.indexes.json

IMPORTANT
These are prototype starting rules, not a legal/security certification.
Run Firebase Emulator tests before deployment.

FIREBASE ROLE

Hosting:
Web PWA.

Auth:
Optional account.

Firestore:
Project directory
Members
Comments
Light sync update envelopes
Asset metadata only.

NOT FIRESTORE
WAV
video
waveform arrays
meter values
playhead frame spam
plugin state blobs
model weights.

COLLECTION SHAPE

users/{uid}

projects/{projectId}
  ownerUserId
  title
  schemaVersion
  modifiedAt
  visibility
  syncRevision

projects/{projectId}/members/{uid}
  role
  permissions

projects/{projectId}/comments/{commentId}

projects/{projectId}/syncUpdates/{updateId}

assets/{assetId}
  projectId
  contentHash
  mediaType
  byteLength

SECURITY
Rules are deny-by-default for undefined collections.
Project member check is server-evaluated.
Rights/release/payment systems should NOT be added casually to client-only
Firestore rules; use audited trusted service paths when introduced.

COST CONTROL
Debounce project-directory modifiedAt updates.
Batch comments/activity where appropriate.
CRDT updates should be compacted.
Do not write transport playback state continuously.

MVP ACCEPTANCE
FB-001 unrelated authenticated user cannot read project.
FB-002 member can read.
FB-003 viewer role cannot create sync update.
FB-004 editor can create update.
FB-005 comment author can edit own comment.
FB-006 anonymous local project needs no Firebase.
FB-007 emulator test suite passes before deploy.

#################### 46_PWA_OFFLINE_BOOT_LOCAL_STORAGE_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.8

46 — PWA, OFFLINE BOOT & LOCAL STORAGE CONTRACT
===============================================

COMPANION
manifest.webmanifest

GOAL
After first successful load/install, core project creation/open/edit/playback must
work with network disabled.

CACHE CLASSES

APP_SHELL
HTML
JS/CSS bundles
icons
essential help
built-in tiny demo resources.

LOCAL_PROJECT_DATA
IndexedDB.

LOCAL_MEDIA
OPFS preferred; IndexedDB Blob fallback.

DERIVED_CACHE
waveform peaks
thumbnails
proxies
analysis.

REMOTE_HTTP_CACHE
Never treated as only canonical copy.

SERVICE WORKER POLICY

App shell:
stale-while-revalidate or versioned cache.

API:
network-first where user expects current remote state, but never blocks local
project.

Media:
do not blindly CacheStorage giant WAV/video.
AssetStore owns media caching.

UPDATE
New service worker waits.
If project recording/performance active:
do not force reload.
Show "Update ready" later.

OFFLINE START
1 load cached shell.
2 open local DB.
3 list local projects.
4 initialize audio.
5 network services enter Offline.
6 user works.

STORAGE PERSISTENCE
Request persistent browser storage where supported after user has meaningful
project data, explaining why if browser UX requires.

QUOTA
StorageService monitors estimated usage where API supports it.
Warn before failure.
Never auto-delete sole asset copy.

EVICTION
Derived cache first.
Downloaded replica next if another verified copy exists.
Never canonical local original without explicit policy.

PWA ACCEPTANCE
PWA-001 first load online.
PWA-002 reload offline.
PWA-003 create project offline.
PWA-004 import WAV offline.
PWA-005 reopen offline.
PWA-006 update does not interrupt recording.
PWA-007 quota error visible.
PWA-008 cache cleanup preserves sole originals.
PWA-009 service worker never caches OAuth callback secrets.
PWA-010 network restoration starts sync asynchronously.

#################### 51_MONOREPO_PACKAGE_BOOTSTRAP.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.9

51 — MONOREPO / PACKAGE BOOTSTRAP
=================================

DELIVERED
README.md
tsconfig.foundation.json
package.integration.example.json
starter folder tree.

MIGRATION APPROACH
Do not convert to a workspace monorepo in the same commit as the first audio
vertical slice unless tooling remains stable.

Safe sequence:
1. create packages/ directories inside existing Vite repo;
2. use relative imports initially;
3. move App/domain state into packages incrementally;
4. only then introduce npm/pnpm workspace aliases if useful.

WHY
The current repository is still a single Vite application. A giant package
manager/workspace migration would create unrelated breakage before persistence and
real audio are proven.

FIRST PACKAGE BOUNDARIES
domain
project
assets
commands
timeline
audio-contracts
audio-web.

No backend package required for local milestone.

BOOT-001 foundation TypeScript compiles strict.
BOOT-002 existing UI may continue importing old modules during migration.
BOOT-003 no circular dependency from domain to React/audio/vendor SDK.

#################### 54_OPFS_ASSET_STORE_HASH_WAVEFORM_CODE.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.9

54 — OPFS ASSET STORE, HASHING & WAVEFORM CODE
==============================================

DELIVERED
AssetStore.ts
WebLocalAssetStore.ts
sha256.ts
waveform.ts
waveform.worker.ts

BEHAVIOR
- compute SHA-256;
- prefer Origin Private File System;
- fallback to IndexedDB Blob;
- runtime asks AssetStore by assetId;
- waveform worker operates on decoded PCM arrays.

IMPORTANT
Audio decoding itself stays outside worker because browser worker audio-decoding
capabilities are inconsistent; decode with audio backend/main analysis service,
then transfer PCM to peak worker.

MEDIA-001 put/get returns same bytes.
MEDIA-002 hash stable.
MEDIA-003 missing returns null.
MEDIA-004 peak pyramid reflects real sample data.
MEDIA-005 deleting cache is distinct from deleting sole original.

====================================

DELIVERED

IMPLEMENTED COMMANDS
AddTrack
SetClipGain
SplitAudioClip.

LIMITATION
The starter inverse system intentionally does not pretend every command is
undoable yet. AddTrack and Split require explicit inverse/transaction records
before production Undo claims complete coverage.

FIRST PRODUCTION TASK
Introduce command record:
before/after or inverse factory
and AddTrack/DeleteTrack + Split/Merge transaction inverse.

RULE
Do not connect Ctrl+Z to the old rack-only history once project CommandBus takes
ownership.

CMDSTART-001 SetClipGain undo/redo persists.
CMDSTART-002 invalid split does not save.
CMDSTART-003 command mutates cloned/immutable project structures.

61 — INSTALLED APP & NATIVE RUNTIME ARCHITECTURE
================================================

REQUIREMENT

Except for the Web App / Website, Poietek runs as an installed operating-system
application.

WINDOWS
Install using signed installer/Microsoft Store route.
Launch from Start Menu/desktop.
Local filesystem/database.
Native audio/MIDI service.
Desktop VST3/CLAP host later.

MACOS
.app bundle + DMG or App Store.
Signed/notarized.
Launch from Applications/Dock.
CoreAudio/CoreMIDI/native filesystem.
AU/VST3/CLAP where permitted.

LINUX
AppImage plus distro packages where supported.
Native audio/MIDI adapters selected by implementation.
VST3/CLAP/LV2 where implemented.

ANDROID
Installed APK/AAB through Google Play/direct supported channels.
Launch from launcher.
Android-native permissions/storage/audio/MIDI adapters.
No claim of desktop VST binaries.

IOS/IPADOS
Installed through App Store/TestFlight/development.
Launch from Home Screen.
Native iOS/iPadOS audio/MIDI/storage integrations.
AUv3 hosting where supported.

WEB
Normal browser.
PWA optional.
Never pretend browser capabilities equal desktop.

WEBSITE
Normal public website.
Separate from realtime studio boot.

TAURI 2

Working shell recommendation:
shared React frontend;
Rust application core;
Swift/Kotlin native integration when required.

The user experience is an app, regardless of shared WebView UI implementation.

NATIVE BRIDGE

File picker
filesystem
secure credentials
windowing
power/sleep
audio device
MIDI
plugin host
camera/mic
share/export
notifications
updates.

RULE
Feature code queries capability service instead of:
if(window.__TAURI__) scattered everywhere.

ACCEPTANCE
NATIVE-001 Windows install/uninstall.
NATIVE-002 macOS signed launch.
NATIVE-003 Linux launch package.
NATIVE-004 Android installed launch.
NATIVE-005 iPad installed launch.
NATIVE-006 installed app works with browser closed.
NATIVE-007 local project files survive app restart.
NATIVE-008 native app can work without account/network.
NATIVE-009 web project opens with capability fallbacks.
NATIVE-010 platform unsupported feature preserved/frozen, not deleted.

OFFICIAL REFERENCE
https://tauri.app/
https://v2.tauri.app/distribute/

#################### 61_NATIVE_INSTALLED_APP_RUNTIME_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 2.0

61 — NATIVE INSTALLED APP RUNTIME CONTRACT
==========================================

PRODUCT RULE

Poietek has two delivery modes:

A. WEB
Runs in a browser as a web application/PWA.

B. INSTALLED APPLICATION
Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS/iPadOS install Poietek as an application and
launch it from the operating system like normal software.

The installed application must NOT require the user to manually open a browser.

SHARED CODE

React/TypeScript UI
Canonical Project format
Commands
Assets
Storage Router
Sync contracts
Design system
Most feature UI.

NATIVE LAYER

Tauri 2 is the recommended shell candidate.

Native layer owns or delegates:
filesystem
native dialogs
app data directories
windowing
system tray where useful
audio device integration
MIDI
plugin hosting
OS keychain
updates
camera/microphone permissions
notifications
share sheets
background capability subject to platform rules.

WEB LAYER

Browser owns:
WebAudio
WebMIDI when available
IndexedDB
OPFS
service worker
browser permissions.

INSTALLED USER EXPERIENCE

Windows:
installer / Microsoft Store option.
Launch from Start Menu/Desktop/Taskbar.
Normal file association later.

macOS:
DMG/App Store option.
Applications folder.
Dock.
Signed/notarized distribution.

Linux:
AppImage/deb/rpm/Snap/AUR choices according to release policy.

Android:
Google Play / signed APK for developer/testing where policy permits.

iOS/iPadOS:
App Store/TestFlight.
Home Screen application.
Native permission prompts.

WEBSITE

poietek.<domain>
marketing
docs
community
downloads
account
web app launch.

The public website and the Web App are separate routes/build concerns even if
they share design components.

NO "WRAPPED WEBSITE" QUALITY BAR

Installed Poietek must behave like an app:
- native file open/save;
- native windowing where available;
- deep links/file associations later;
- correct sleep/background behavior;
- native menus/shortcuts where appropriate;
- reliable offline startup;
- app-specific local storage;
- installer/update lifecycle.

The UI may be web technology, but the product behavior must be native-quality.

PLATFORM CAPABILITY

Same project file does NOT mean same binary capabilities.

Desktop:
full plugin host
advanced audio I/O
large projects
multi-window.

Tablet:
touch-first
AUv3 on Apple where implemented
external interfaces/controllers.

Phone:
capture/edit/review/compact production/live mode.

Web:
maximum portable capability
no claim of arbitrary native desktop plugin hosting.

INSTALLATION ACCEPTANCE

NATIVE-001 Windows installer creates launchable app.
NATIVE-002 macOS signed test build launches from Applications.
NATIVE-003 Linux package launches without browser.
NATIVE-004 Android app launches from launcher.
NATIVE-005 iOS/iPadOS app launches from Home Screen.
NATIVE-006 installed app opens offline.
NATIVE-007 installed app uses application data directory.
NATIVE-008 uninstall does not silently delete user projects outside app-managed data.
NATIVE-009 crash does not launch external browser as fallback.
NATIVE-010 web version remains usable without native install.

#################### 62_PLATFORM_CAPABILITY_INSTALLATION_MATRIX.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 2.0

62 — PLATFORM CAPABILITY & INSTALLATION MATRIX
==============================================

Companion:
platform-capability-matrix.csv

PRINCIPLE

Project compatibility is universal.
Runtime capability is platform-specific.

A project containing an unavailable desktop plug-in still opens on mobile/web:
the plug-in state remains and the last freeze/render is used where available.

The app never deletes unsupported state just because the current platform cannot
execute it.

CAPABILITY SERVICE

PlatformCapabilities {
  nativeInstalled
  audioBackend
  midi
  pluginFormats[]
  videoAcceleration
  filesystemMode
  multiWindow
  camera
  microphone
  backgroundTasks
  shareSheet
  notifications
}

UI uses capability checks, not user-agent string guesses.

ACCEPTANCE
PLAT-001 unsupported plugin shown as unavailable/frozen.
PLAT-002 web never advertises VST3 hosting.
PLAT-003 tablet layout is touch-first.
PLAT-004 desktop supports detach/multi-window after implementation.
PLAT-005 same project ID/schema opens all supported platforms.

#################### 63_SUPABASE_FIREBASE_HYBRID_BACKEND.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.10

63 — SUPABASE + FIREBASE HYBRID BACKEND
=======================================

RECOMMENDED PROTOTYPE

LOCAL CLIENT
Creative source of truth while working.
IndexedDB/OPFS web.
SQLite/files native.

SUPABASE
Canonical hosted backend:
Auth
Postgres
teams/project directory
comments
rights metadata later
Realtime collaboration relay/presence.

FIREBASE
Non-overlapping optional services:
Hosting for Web App/Website
Cloud Messaging where appropriate
Remote Config / staged feature flags
App Check where appropriate
other Firebase diagnostics/platform services after privacy review.

MEDIA
Local + BYOC.
Supabase free Storage may handle small thumbnails/previews, but is not the large
multitrack media strategy.

WHY NOT DOUBLE WRITE?
Writing every project record to Firestore and Postgres creates:
conflicts
cost
migration problems
two authorization models
two failure paths.

CanonicalBackend interface selects one hosted data authority.

FIREBASE-ONLY TEST MODE
Allowed.
Implement FirebaseBackendAdapter and disable Supabase adapter.

SUPABASE-ONLY MODE
Allowed.

SELF-HOSTED MODE
Later.

CURRENT FREE-TIER SNAPSHOT — AUGUST 2026
Supabase Free currently advertises:
50,000 MAU
500 MB DB
5 GB egress
1 GB file storage
200 peak realtime connections
2 million realtime messages/month
50 MB max file upload
and free projects may pause after one week inactivity.

Firebase Spark documentation currently provides no-cost usage for many Firebase
services and daily Firestore quota such as 20k writes/50k reads in its example.

Always re-check before release.

OFFICIAL SOURCES
https://supabase.com/pricing
https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth
https://firebase.google.com/docs/projects/billing/firebase-pricing-plans

#################### 63_SUPABASE_FIREBASE_HYBRID_BACKEND_STRATEGY.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 2.0

63 — SUPABASE + FIREBASE HYBRID BACKEND STRATEGY
=================================================

RECOMMENDED FREE-FIRST DEFAULT

PRIMARY APPLICATION BACKEND:
Supabase.

Supabase responsibilities:
- Auth
- Postgres application database
- Row Level Security
- Realtime presence/broadcast
- comments/team metadata
- project directory
- rights/release metadata later
- small managed file objects if desired.

FIREBASE OPTIONAL RESPONSIBILITIES:
- Firebase Hosting for the Web build if desired
- Crashlytics / Performance Monitoring for supported app surfaces
- Remote Config
- Cloud Messaging later
- App Distribution/testing where useful.

LOCAL/BYOC:
- project source of truth
- WAV/video
- local autosave
- user-connected storage providers.

WHY NOT TWO DATABASE SOURCES OF TRUTH

Do not put project membership in both Firestore and Supabase and try to merge it.

One responsibility -> one owner.

MVP RECOMMENDATION

Supabase:
users
projects
members
comments
presence
sync metadata.

Firebase:
optional Hosting only at first.

This is much simpler than running Firebase Auth + Supabase Auth simultaneously.

FIREBASE AUTH COMPATIBILITY

Supabase currently supports Firebase Auth as a third-party JWT identity source.
Therefore a future migration/hybrid path exists.

Use that only if:
- Firebase Auth is already established;
- custom claim/security setup is tested;
- one canonical user identity mapping is documented.

Do NOT enable both auth systems casually during first prototype.

SUPABASE LOCAL DEVELOPMENT

Use Supabase CLI + local Postgres/Auth/Realtime for backend development.
Migrations live in source control.

SELF HOSTING

Supported as a future advanced/team/enterprise mode, with the warning that
self-hosting transfers operations, backup, security, availability and updates to
the operator.

BACKEND ADAPTER

BackendProvider {
 auth
 projectDirectory
 teams
 comments
 presence
 syncRelay
 notifications
}

Implement:
SupabaseBackendProvider
FirebaseBackendProvider (optional)
LocalOnlyBackendProvider.

LOCAL ONLY

LocalOnlyBackendProvider:
anonymous local identity
no network project directory
no team sync
all DAW functions operational.

ACCEPTANCE
BACKEND-001 Supabase unavailable -> local project still opens.
BACKEND-002 Firebase Hosting unavailable -> installed apps unaffected.
BACKEND-003 no project table duplicated in Firestore and Postgres by default.
BACKEND-004 auth provider switch does not change Project schema.
BACKEND-005 local development can run without hosted Supabase.

#################### 64_SUPABASE_MVP_SCHEMA_RLS_CONTRACT.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 2.0

64 — SUPABASE MVP SCHEMA + RLS CONTRACT
======================================

COMPANION
supabase/migrations/0001_poietek_mvp.sql

TABLES
profiles
projects
project_members
comments.

This is intentionally smaller than the future full backend.

NO REMOTE PCM/VIDEO IN DATABASE.

RLS
Every project and comment query must be protected by membership.
Client role text never bypasses database policy.

FIRST SIGN-UP FLOW
Auth user
-> profile
-> create project directory
-> insert owner membership
-> local Project remains canonical creative document.

REALTIME
Use Broadcast/Presence for ephemeral collaboration.
Use database rows for durable comments/project directory.

CRDT
Do not push every CRDT byte into project row.
Dedicated sync channel/service comes later.

ACCEPTANCE
SUPA-001 stranger cannot select private project.
SUPA-002 owner can update.
SUPA-003 viewer cannot edit project directory.
SUPA-004 member can comment.
SUPA-005 local-only project needs no row.

#################### 65_FIVE_STAR_COMPETITIVE_BENCHMARK_STANDARD.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.10

65 — FIVE-STAR COMPETITIVE BENCHMARK STANDARD
=============================================

PURPOSE

The creator requires Poietek to target five-star quality against products with
similar functions.

IMPORTANT

A five-star score is NOT awarded because the specification contains a feature.
It requires reproducible implementation evidence.

STAR SCALE

1
Prototype / critical capabilities missing.

2
Basic usable implementation, materially behind professional references.

3
Competitive baseline for ordinary professional use.

4
Strong professional implementation, at or near best references.

5
Best-in-class release gate:
meets or exceeds the strongest relevant reference for the defined benchmark and
has no critical reliability/usability regression.

COMPARISON PRINCIPLE

Poietek is not compared to one product.

For each module compare against category leaders:

DAW/Live:
Ableton Live
Logic Pro
Pro Tools
FL Studio
Studio One where applicable.

Pitch/time:
Melodyne
Auto-Tune
Revoice
DAW-native pitch tools.

DJ:
Serato
rekordbox
Traktor
VirtualDJ
djay
Mixxx.

Video/VFX:
DaVinci Resolve
Premiere/After Effects-class workflows where relevant.

Synth/modulation/effects:
Serum/Vital
high-quality commercial/open references.

Cloud/collaboration:
current creator tools plus Git-like versioning references.

CATEGORIES
See benchmark-rubric.v1.json.

5-STAR CERTIFICATION RULE

No category below 4.5.
Weighted total >= 4.8.
Audio Engine = 5.
Offline/Local = 5.
Stability = 5.
Installed Platform behavior = 5.
No P0/P1 data loss/security/audio-dropout bug.
Every score linked to evidence.

PUBLIC CLAIM
Do not market "5-Star Certified" until independent/reproducible test records exist.

RETEST
Every major release.
Competitor reference versions recorded.

#################### 66_COMPETITIVE_FEATURE_BENCHMARK_2026.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 1.10

66 — COMPETITIVE FEATURE BENCHMARK — AUGUST 2026
================================================

TYPE OF TEST

This is a published-capability/design benchmark based on first-party product
documentation, combined with the audited current Poietek/SDS implementation.

It is NOT a lab measurement of competitor latency, DSP transparency or crash rate.

REFERENCE FINDINGS

ABLETON LIVE
Current Live materials reinforce:
Arrangement + Session workflows
audio/MIDI recording
takes/comping
warping
audio-to-MIDI
native instruments/effects
third-party plugins
stem separation.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
Match those professional DAW/live capabilities, then differentiate with native
hardware mapping, DJ/video/rights/storage integration.

LOGIC PRO
Apple's current Logic material reinforces:
professional Mac/iPad creation
Session Players
Stem Splitter
advanced creation/mixing.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
Installed tablet workflow cannot be a toy companion.

PRO TOOLS
Current Avid material demonstrates:
professional editing/mixing
Dolby Atmos/internal renderer workflows
ARA/Melodyne-related integration
professional post-production expectations.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
Professional recording/editing, routing and immersive workflows need measurable
accuracy before five stars.

FL STUDIO
Current Image-Line materials reinforce:
fast pattern/beat workflow
audio recording
stem separation
large native instrument/effect family
plugin support
lifetime-update value proposition.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
Beatmaking must remain fast and immediate despite broader architecture.

SERATO
Current Serato DJ Pro documents:
4 decks
stems
100+ supported hardware devices
FX
sampler
DVS expansion
club hardware workflows.

REKORDBOX
Current rekordbox documents:
library/set preparation
STEMS
cloud library sync
DVS
video
USB export/library workflows.

DJAY
Current Algoriddim documentation confirms installed operation across:
macOS
iOS/iPad
Windows
Android
with a cross-device account and platform-dependent capabilities.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
Installed cross-platform creative app behavior is a competitive requirement, not
only a technical preference.

DAVINCI RESOLVE
Current Resolve 21 documentation presents:
editing
color
Fusion VFX/motion graphics
Fairlight audio
media/delivery
multi-user collaboration
in one application.
Fairlight claims up to 2,000 realtime audio tracks in its published feature page.

POIETEK REQUIREMENT
The video/VFX ambition needs a dedicated GPU/proxy/render architecture; a basic
video timeline is not five stars.

#################### 66_FIVE_STAR_COMPETITIVE_BENCHMARK_FRAMEWORK.txt ####################

POIETEK IMPLEMENTATION BATCH — VERSION 2.0

66 — 5-STAR COMPETITIVE BENCHMARK FRAMEWORK
===========================================

COMPANIONS
benchmark-rubric.json
poietek-5-star-benchmark.csv

CRITICAL RULE

"Make Poietek 5 stars" is a RELEASE TARGET, not a score we can award before the
software exists.

A false 5-star score would weaken the product.

STAR STANDARD

1★
Prototype / concept.

2★
Basic end-to-end feature works.

3★
Production-usable ordinary workflow.

4★
Competitive with established specialist/category leaders.

5★
Meets or exceeds the best relevant reference on agreed objective tests,
reliability, workflow efficiency and user validation.

CATEGORY LEADERSHIP MODEL

Poietek does not need one competitor that matches its entire scope.
Benchmark each subsystem against the strongest specialist reference:

DAW/arrangement:
Ableton Live
Logic Pro
FL Studio
Bitwig Studio.

Plugin sandbox/modulation:
Bitwig.

Pitch/note editing:
Melodyne.

DJ/DVS:
Serato DJ Pro
rekordbox.

Video/VFX/audio post:
DaVinci Resolve.

Touch/tablet:
Logic Pro for iPad.

Extensibility:
Max for Live
plugin standards.

5-STAR EVIDENCE

Every 5★ category requires:
feature acceptance suite
performance benchmark
stability/soak
platform matrix
data-loss test
accessibility/usability test
comparison workflow script
version/date of reference competitor
evidence artifact.

NO "FEATURE COUNT" GAMING

Having 200 shallow functions does not beat a competitor's 20 reliable ones.

Weight:
Reliability 25%
Workflow/UX 20%
Output quality 20%
Capability depth 15%
Performance 10%
Interoperability 5%
Accessibility 5%

A critical data-loss/security failure caps category at 2★ until fixed.

RELEASE GATES

Alpha:
may contain 1–3★ experimental modules if labelled.

Beta:
core DAW/local project categories >=3★.

Stable 1.0:
launch-critical categories >=4★.

POIETEK 5-STAR CERTIFIED:
all declared flagship categories = 5★ with evidence.

If the owner's requirement is literally 5★ across all declared categories, do
not badge/declare the release until every category passes.

BENCH-001 score cannot be edited to 5 without evidence IDs.
BENCH-002 competitor version/date recorded.
BENCH-003 performance compared on controlled hardware where meaningful.
BENCH-004 subjective UX includes blinded/structured user testing.
BENCH-005 failures generate gap tickets.

68 — 5-STAR GAP CLOSURE PROGRAM
===============================

PROGRAM RULE

A module enters the 5-Star Program only after it has a real end-to-end implementation.

TRACK A — FOUNDATION

Project durability
Audio playback
recording
render
offline
crash recovery.

Target first.

TRACK B — NATIVE

Windows/macOS/Linux install.
Then iPad/iOS/Android.

TRACK C — MUSIC

MIDI
sampler
arrangement
mixer
automation
synth/effects
plugins.

TRACK D — SPECIALIST

pitch
DJ
video/VFX.

TRACK E — NETWORK

sync
collaboration
community
rights.

TARGET PROCESS FOR EACH CATEGORY

1 Establish category leader/reference version.
2 Write 10–30 workflow tests.
3 Write objective performance/output tests.
4 Implement.
5 Internal score.
6 External creator test.
7 Close gaps.
8 Re-test.
9 Evidence review.
10 Award star.

5★ cannot be inherited from another module.

EXAMPLE: PLUGIN HOST

Reference strength:
Bitwig separate-process crash protection.

Poietek 5★ requirement:
- VST3/CLAP;
- plugin scan isolation;
- configurable process isolation;
- crash cannot kill project;
- state recovery;
- PDC;
- sidechain/multiout;
- freeze portability;
- soak test;
- measurable startup/scan speed;
- accessible missing-plugin UI.

EXAMPLE: TABLET

Reference strength:
Logic Pro iPad touch-first design and Mac/iPad project interoperability.

Poietek 5★ requirement:
- touch-first;
- stylus;
- external MIDI/audio;
- same project;
- offline;
- no desktop plugin deletion;
- native app launch;
- performance appropriate to device;
- real user testing.

PROGRAM OUTPUT
benchmark/evidence/<category>/<version>/
  reference.md
  workflow-results.json
  performance.json
  screenshots/
  crash-soak.json
  user-test-summary.md
  score.json

69 — NATIVE RUNTIME / PLATFORM CAPABILITY CODE
==============================================

COMPANION
packages/platform/src/PlatformCapabilities.ts
src-tauri/tauri.conf.example.json

WHY

Feature UI should ask:
"Can this runtime do VST3?"
not:
"Is user agent Windows?"

Native shell injects authoritative platform capability data.

WEB
webCapabilities() performs only browser capability discovery.

TAURI
A future TauriPlatformCapabilities adapter obtains:
OS
app version
native bridge features
permissions.

FEATURE FLAGS
Experimental feature flag != platform capability.
Keep separate.

ACCEPTANCE

CAP-002 mobile does not inherit desktop VST flags.
CAP-003 installed app marked installedNativeApp.
CAP-004 missing capability disables feature with explanation.

#################### 81_HYBRID_BLOCKCHAIN_LEDGER_ARCHITECTURE.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

81 — HYBRID BLOCKCHAIN LEDGER ARCHITECTURE
==========================================

GOAL

Add a tamper-evident blockchain/smart-contract layer that can prove when a
specific version of a Poietek rights/provenance/settlement record was anchored.

DO NOT PUT THE WHOLE DATABASE ON CHAIN.

PRIVATE OFF-CHAIN LEDGER
stores:
full Party identity
PRS/PPL/MLC/SoundExchange IDs
contracts
banking/tax
emails
private splits
full statements
private project history.

OPTIONAL PUBLIC CHAIN
stores:
hashes
Merkle roots
version
kind
block number
block timestamp
signer/contract address where appropriate
supersession links.

BENEFIT

A later verifier can prove:
"This exact Rights Manifest hash existed in this anchored state."

It does not prove:
the legal truth of every claim inside the document.

DUAL LEDGER

Operational Ledger:
Postgres/local database.

Evidence Ledger:
blockchain anchors.

The operational ledger remains usable if blockchain is unavailable.

ACCEPTANCE
CHAIN-001 no blockchain required to record session.
CHAIN-002 anchoring failure does not block local project.
CHAIN-003 private IDs absent from public transaction.
CHAIN-004 manifest verification catches altered content.

#################### 82_TRIPLE_TIME_PROOF_AND_EVENT_CHRONOLOGY.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

82 — TRIPLE TIME PROOF & EVENT CHRONOLOGY
=========================================

USER GOAL

Track time/date of:
session
contribution
rights approval
final master
registration
sale
royalty statement
payout
manifest changes.

DO NOT USE BLOCK TIMESTAMP AS A PRECISION CLOCK.

Ethereum/Solidity exposes block.timestamp, but official Solidity documentation
warns block timestamps can be influenced to some degree.

POIETEK USES THREE TIME SOURCES

1 SIGNED EVENT TIME
Device/service UTC timestamp included in canonical event.

2 SERVER/AUDIT RECEIPT
Poietek backend records receipt time.

3 BLOCKCHAIN INCLUSION
block number + block timestamp + transaction confirmation.

Optional fourth:
independent RFC3161/transparency/timestamp service.

EventChronology {
 eventId
 eventType
 actorPartyId
 signedEventTime
 serverReceivedAt
 chainIncludedAt?
 blockNumber?
 contentHash
 previousEventHash?
}

For recording sessions, audio hardware clock remains the media timing authority;
the blockchain is not a sample clock.

ACCEPTANCE
TIME-001 all stored times use UTC internally.
TIME-002 UI renders local timezone.
TIME-003 chain time labelled "block inclusion time".
TIME-004 event alteration changes hash.

#################### 83_CONTRIBUTOR_CRYPTOGRAPHIC_APPROVALS_EIP712.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

83 — CONTRIBUTOR CRYPTOGRAPHIC APPROVALS
========================================

COMPANIONS
contracts/PoietekRightsApprovalRegistry.sol
packages/blockchain/src/RightsApprovalTypedData.ts

PURPOSE

A contributor can approve a particular version/hash of:
credit
rights claim
split proposal
final Rights Manifest.

EIP-712

Use typed structured-data signing so a wallet/user agent can display meaningful
fields rather than an opaque random byte string.

ERC-1271 / SignatureChecker

Support smart-contract wallet signatures as well as ordinary account signatures.

APPROVAL OBJECT

manifest ID hash
manifest content hash
Party ID hash
approval role
version
nonce
expiry.

WHY NONCE

Prevents a previously signed authorization from being reused as a fresh approval.

WHY VERSION/HASH

Approving version 3 does not approve a later changed version 4.

NO WALLET REQUIREMENT FOR EVERY USER

Poietek account/passkey signatures can remain a valid off-chain approval path.

Blockchain approval is optional or used for higher-assurance workflows.

Account abstraction can later make wallet UX less intimidating and potentially
allow Poietek/paymasters to sponsor gas rather than forcing users to hold native
tokens.

ACCEPTANCE
SIGN-001 changed manifest invalidates prior version match.
SIGN-002 expired approval rejected.
SIGN-003 nonce prevents replay.
SIGN-004 smart-contract wallet validation supported.
SIGN-005 no seed phrase required for ordinary Poietek local workflow.

#################### 84_SMART_CONTRACT_ANCHOR_REGISTRY.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

84 — SMART-CONTRACT ANCHOR REGISTRY
===================================

COMPANION
contracts/PoietekLedgerAnchor.sol
packages/blockchain/src/BlockchainAnchorProvider.ts

ANCHOR KINDS

RightsManifest
ContributorApprovalBatch
RegistrationReceiptBatch
MarketplaceSettlementBatch
ExternalRoyaltyStatementBatch
ReleaseSnapshot
ProvenanceSnapshot.

ANCHOR ID

Deterministically or randomly generated unique record key.

FIELDS

objectIdHash
contentHash
metadataRoot
version
kind
submittedBy
blockNumber
blockTimestamp.

NO PII

Party IDs should normally be salted/hashed or represented through privacy-aware
commitments before public anchoring.

SUPERSESSION

Historical anchor is never deleted.
A new anchor may declare that it supersedes an earlier version.

ADMIN

Production deployments should use audited role management, multisig/smart-account
administration and delayed admin changes.

The starter contract uses OpenZeppelin AccessControlDefaultAdminRules and Pausable
as a design scaffold, not as a completed security audit.

ACCEPTANCE
ANCHOR-001 duplicate anchor ID rejected.
ANCHOR-002 pause stops new anchors.
ANCHOR-003 old anchor remains queryable.
ANCHOR-004 supersession creates event rather than mutation.

#################### 85_SETTLEMENT_MERKLE_ROOT_PAYMENT_ROYALTY_PROOFS.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

85 — SETTLEMENT MERKLE ROOTS & PAYMENT/ROYALTY PROOFS
=====================================================

COMPANION
packages/blockchain/src/SettlementMerkle.ts
database/blockchain-anchor-ledger.v1.sql

PROBLEM

Thousands of payment/royalty lines are expensive and privacy-invasive to store
directly on chain.

SOLUTION

Keep private settlement lines in Poietek's normal ledger.
Create a Merkle tree over canonical allocation records.
Anchor only the Merkle root.

Each contributor can receive:
their settlement line
Merkle inclusion proof
batch anchor receipt.

This can prove the contributor's line was included in the anchored batch without
publishing every contributor's private payment details.

COVERED SOURCES

Poietek marketplace sales
tips/support
licensing
imported PRS/PPL/MLC/SoundExchange statements
distribution statements
other royalty statements.

IMPORTANT

For an external royalty statement, Poietek anchors what the statement says.
It does not make the blockchain the payer or change the society's legal/payment
process.

FIAT PAYMENT

Payment processor/bank receipt remains off-chain.
Hash receipt into settlement evidence.

ON-CHAIN PAYMENT

Future optional mode may settle eligible transactions with tokenized funds or
stable-value rails, but requires legal, tax, custody, sanctions and smart-contract
audit review before production.

ACCEPTANCE
SET-001 altered settlement line fails inclusion proof.
SET-002 private line not publicly readable.
SET-003 source statement hash retained.
SET-004 fiat processor receipt linked by hash.

#################### 86_ONCHAIN_VS_OFFCHAIN_PAYMENT_ROUTING.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

86 — ON-CHAIN VS OFF-CHAIN PAYMENT ROUTING
==========================================

PAYMENT ROUTER

Transaction can use:
Stripe Connect
Apple payment rail
Google Play Billing
bank transfer
PayPal/other marketplace processor
future compliant on-chain rail.

RIGHTS GRAPH stays independent of payment rail.

OFF-CHAIN FIAT

Best early choice for ordinary marketplace users.

Benefits:
card/bank UX
refunds/chargebacks
KYC/payout infrastructure
ordinary currency.

Blockchain:
anchor settlement receipts/roots only.

ON-CHAIN

Optional later for:
web3-native marketplace items
public grants
creator collectives
machine-to-machine licensing
transparent community pools.

Do not force crypto wallets on ordinary artists/listeners.

CUSTODY

Poietek should avoid holding user funds directly where a regulated processor can
handle them.

If a smart contract holds money:
independent audit
reentrancy review
emergency/pause policy
upgrade policy
lost-key recovery design
sanctions/AML/tax/legal review
must precede release.

ACCEPTANCE
PAY-001 rights split same regardless of payment rail.
PAY-002 fiat user never needs wallet.
PAY-003 blockchain unavailable does not block card sale.
PAY-004 on-chain payout feature disabled until audit/legal gate.

#################### 87_PRIVACY_GDPR_IMMUTABILITY_AND_RIGHT_TO_CORRECTION.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

87 — PRIVACY, IMMUTABILITY & RIGHTS CORRECTION
==============================================

BLOCKCHAINS ARE HARD TO ERASE.

Therefore never write:
legal name unless specifically public/appropriate
email
home address
tax ID
bank details
private society login/member secret
private contracts
unreleased lyrics/audio
private messages.

USE HASHES/COMMITMENTS.

RIGHT TO CORRECTION

Blockchain record isn't edited.

Example:
Manifest v1 anchored.
Contributor spelling/split later corrected.
Create Manifest v2.
All required people approve v2.
Anchor v2.
Record v1 -> superseded by v2.

The history remains auditable while current application truth points to v2.

HASH PRIVACY

Naively hashing low-entropy personal data can still leak via guessing.

Use:
random/salted identifiers
commitments
or avoid anchoring personal fields entirely.

ACCEPTANCE
PRIVCHAIN-001 delete account can remove off-chain PII.
PRIVCHAIN-002 public chain contains no raw email.
PRIVCHAIN-003 correction generates new version.
PRIVCHAIN-004 UI clearly shows current vs historical manifest.

#################### 88_WALLETLESS_UX_ACCOUNT_ABSTRACTION_GAS_SPONSORSHIP.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

88 — WALLETLESS UX, SMART ACCOUNTS & GAS SPONSORSHIP
=====================================================

PRODUCT RULE

Users should not need to understand:
seed phrases
gas
chain IDs
RPCs
wallet networks

to record a song or receive ordinary royalties.

LEVEL 0
No blockchain account.
Poietek operates off-chain/local.

LEVEL 1
Poietek anchors evidence on behalf of user/service.
User sees verification receipt.

LEVEL 2
User links an existing wallet if desired.

LEVEL 3
Smart-account/account-abstraction UX.
Programmable recovery, batching and sponsored gas may improve experience.

Ethereum's current account-abstraction direction explicitly supports smart-contract
wallets and can enable applications/paymasters to sponsor transaction fees.

KEY RECOVERY

Never make a contributor lose rights records because a wallet key is lost.

Poietek Party identity remains canonical.
Wallets are linked proof/signing methods that can be rotated under audited policy.

ACCEPTANCE
WALLET-001 ordinary user can ignore blockchain.
WALLET-002 lost wallet does not delete rights record.
WALLET-003 wallet rotation audited.
WALLET-004 sponsored gas cost tracked by platform.

#################### 89_BLOCKCHAIN_SECURITY_AUDIT_TESTING_RELEASE_GATE.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.2

89 — BLOCKCHAIN SECURITY, AUDIT & RELEASE GATE
==============================================

SMART CONTRACT CODE IS FINANCIAL/SECURITY CRITICAL.

BEFORE MAINNET/PRODUCTION

Static analysis.
Unit tests.
Fuzz tests.
Invariant tests.
Fork/integration tests.
Replay tests.
Role/admin tests.
Pause/recovery tests.
Gas grief tests.
Signature expiry/nonce tests.
Independent smart-contract audit.
Bug bounty when appropriate.

OPENZEPPELIN

Use mature primitives such as:
EIP712
SignatureChecker
AccessControl / AccessManager
Pausable
Nonces
MerkleProof

rather than home-grown cryptography/access control.

UPGRADES

Prefer minimum immutable surface.

If upgradeable:
document admin
timelock
migration
storage layout
upgrade authorization
user notification.

NETWORK ADAPTER

No business logic hard-coded to one chain.

BlockchainAnchorProvider implementations can target different compatible evidence
networks based on cost, reliability and policy.

CHAIN SELECTION

Do not pick a chain based only on cheap gas.
Review:
security
finality
availability
tooling
ecosystem
fees
privacy
regulatory/product requirements
long-term survival.

RELEASE GATE

No contract that can transfer user money is enabled merely because unit tests pass.

ACCEPTANCE
SECCHAIN-001 independent audit required for custody contract.
SECCHAIN-002 signatures include nonce/expiry/domain.
SECCHAIN-003 admin key isn't single developer hot wallet in production.
SECCHAIN-004 emergency pause tested.

Poietek separates NETWORK CONSENSUS from APPLICATION PROOFS.

Customization
Trust.

A non-standard phrase is never used as a security primitive until a precise,

#################### 92_POIETEK_CREATOR_PROOF_REGISTRY.txt ####################

92 — CREATOR PROOF REGISTRY
===========================

COMPANIONS
schemas/proof-record.schema.v1.json
packages/proofs/src/ProofService.ts

PROOF OF CONTRIBUTION
Party + project + session + role + track/clip/object + timestamp + content/version
hash + approval/signature where appropriate.

Contribution does not automatically equal ownership.

PROOF OF CONSENT
Binds a Party to a precise Rights Manifest/version/policy scope.

PROOF OF IDENTITY
Uses Contributor Passport plus provider/KYC/passkey/ZK verification where useful.
Raw identity documents remain private.

PROOF OF DATA INTEGRITY
SHA-256, signatures, C2PA, Merkle roots and immutable audit records.

PROOF OF TRANSACTION HISTORY
Order + Commerce Policy version + ledger + processor receipt + settlement proof.

PROOF OF INTEROPERABILITY
Automated evidence that a project/file/provider round-trips without critical loss.

PROOF OF CHAOS
Evidence from deliberate network/provider/storage/worker/payment failure tests.

PROOF OF TRUST
A transparent bundle of underlying evidence.
Never a mysterious social-credit score controlling rights or money.

94 — GLOBAL PRIVACY, SECURITY & COMPLIANCE ARCHITECTURE
=======================================================

COMPANION
privacy-security-compliance-matrix.csv

Poietek uses jurisdiction/capability modules rather than claiming every law applies
to every user.

PRIVACY ENGINE
Data inventory
purpose
lawful/authorized basis
retention
region
processor/provider
export
delete
correct
restrict/opt-out
consent/version
sensitive classification.

CORE PRODUCT RULE
Creative media is not analytics.
Rights/contracts are not marketing data.
Payment/KYC data is isolated from public/social data.

GDPR/UK STYLE
privacy by design, transparency, minimization, access/correction/deletion and
portable user-data workflows where scope requires.

CCPA/CPRA STYLE
notice, know/delete/correct, opt-out of sale/sharing and sensitive-information

Adopt NIST CSF 2.0 now for governance/risk structure.

SECURE DEVELOPMENT

AI GOVERNANCE
Map AI systems to NIST AI RMF and GenAI Profile.

PAYMENTS
Minimize PCI DSS scope by keeping raw cardholder data with compliant payment rails.

ASSURANCE TARGETS
ISO/IEC 27001 and SOC 2 are future hosted-service assurance targets, not immediate
claims.

HIPAA/GLBA
Conditional only if Poietek enters those regulated scopes.

#################### 97_DAO_DEFI_NFT_ORDINALS_RUNES_OPCAT_CLASSIFICATION.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.3

97 — DAO, DEFI, NFT, ORDINALS, RUNES & OP_CAT CLASSIFICATION
============================================================

DAO
ADAPT LATER.

Possible:
community grants;
open-source roadmap voting;
extension/device-profile stewardship;
creator collective treasury proposals.

Never use token votes to override:
copyright contracts;
contributor approvals;
privacy rights;
legal obligations;
individual payout entitlements.

DEFI
RESEARCH ONLY.

No lending/yield/liquidity-pool system belongs in the core creative app.
Any future financial protocol requires separate legal/security/product review.

NFT / DIGITAL COLLECTIBLE
OPTIONAL.

Possible:
collectible edition;
access pass;
event ticket;
proof-linked digital edition;
license receipt.

Token ownership never automatically equals copyright ownership.

BITCOIN ORDINAL INSCRIPTIONS
RESEARCH/OPTIONAL EXPORT.

Inscriptions can hold arbitrary content/metadata as Bitcoin-native digital
artifacts. Poietek may someday export an approved public collectible/provenance
artifact, but should not put private Rights Manifests or large studio masters
on-chain by default.

RUNES / RUNESTONES
RESEARCH ONLY.

Runes are fungible Bitcoin-native digital commodities with protocol messages
encoded as runestones. Possible future community/collectible experiments are
separate from royalties and legal rights.

OP_CAT
DO NOT DEPEND ON IT.

BIP 347 proposes OP_CAT in Tapscript, but current Bitcoin Core still treats OP_CAT
as disabled. Poietek must not build product-critical functionality on an
unactivated consensus proposal.

ETFs
REJECT FROM CORE.

An exchange-traded fund is a regulated investment product, not a creator-app
feature. If Poietek ever enters investment/securities products it is a separate
regulated business.

END DOCUMENT 97

virtual studio;

#################### 101_UNIVERSAL_CONSOLE_BRIDGE_ARCHITECTURE.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

101 — UNIVERSAL CONSOLE BRIDGE ARCHITECTURE
===========================================

GOAL

A supported mixing desk can appear inside Poietek as one logical mixer.

Physical desk movement:
-> ConsoleAdapter
-> normalized parameter event
-> Mixer State Mirror
-> Poietek UI updates.

Poietek control movement:
-> command/automation
-> Mixer State Mirror
-> ConsoleAdapter
-> physical desk parameter changes when hardware supports remote writing.

SEPARATE PLANES

AUDIO PLANE
actual audio channels.

CLOCK PLANE
sample synchronization.

CONTROL PLANE
faders, mutes, EQ, sends, routing, scenes.

METER PLANE
high-rate telemetry.

TRANSPORT/TIMECODE PLANE
play/stop/locate/time reference.

These may use different cables/protocols on the same desk.

Example:
MADI fibre carries audio/clock.
Ethernet carries MIDI/vendor control.
Word Clock provides house sync.

CAPABILITY NEGOTIATION

Poietek never assumes every digital desk supports every parameter.
A ConsoleProfile declares verified capabilities.

SUPPORTED CLASSES

digital console
analogue console
hybrid console
control surface
stagebox
network bridge
automation bridge.

END DOCUMENT 101

#################### 102_AUDIO_CLOCK_TIMING_SYNC_DOMAINS.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

102 — AUDIO CLOCK, TIMING & SYNC DOMAINS
========================================

SAMPLE CLOCK

Digital audio devices must agree on the sampling frequency or be isolated through
sample-rate conversion.

Poietek models explicit Clock Domains.

ONE LEADER PER SYNCHRONOUS DOMAIN.

Possible sources:
internal
Word Clock
Dante PTP
AES67 PTPv2
MADI embedded
ADAT embedded
AES3 embedded
AES50 embedded
external house clock.

DANTE

Current Dante documentation states that devices use IEEE 1588 PTP and synchronize
to a leader clock with sample-accurate network alignment.

AES67/ST 2110 interoperation can use PTPv2 where supported.

MADI / OPTICAL

Optical is a physical medium, not one protocol.

Optical connection may be:
MADI fibre
ADAT optical
other optical transport.

Poietek identifies the protocol, not merely "optical cable."

MADI is a point-to-point multichannel digital audio interface and may run over
coaxial or fibre; real interfaces can clock from MADI, Word Clock or a network
clock.

AES50

AES50 defines bidirectional multichannel digital audio over Category-5-style
cabling with audio synchronization carried with the connection.

USB

USB audio clock behavior is adapter/device-specific.
Poietek treats the selected native/Web audio device backend as an endpoint and
reports actual clock/lock capabilities.

SRC

If two domains cannot share clock, an explicit sample-rate-conversion bridge may
connect them.

TRANSPORT IS NOT SAMPLE CLOCK

MIDI Clock/MTC/MMC/timecode controls do not replace the audio sampling clock.

SYNC UI

Audio Settings > Clock:

Domain: Studio Main
Sample Rate: 48 kHz
Leader: Console
Poietek Interface: Locked
MADI Bridge: Locked
Dante Network: Locked
Video Interface: SRC
Warnings: none.

CLOCK-001 no accidental two leaders.
CLOCK-002 loss of lock creates visible warning.
CLOCK-003 clock-source change never silently proceeds during recording.
CLOCK-004 PTP/WordClock/etc are capability-based.
CLOCK-005 SRC boundary shown clearly.

END DOCUMENT 102

#################### 103_DIGITAL_CONSOLE_BIDIRECTIONAL_CONTROL.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

103 — DIGITAL CONSOLE BIDIRECTIONAL CONTROL
===========================================

DIGITAL DESK TARGET

Supported parameters may include:
channel name/color
fader
mute
solo
pan
preamp gain
phantom power
polarity
HPF
EQ
gate
compressor
insert
send levels
bus/DCA assignments
routing
scene
transport
meters.

ACTUAL SET DEPENDS ON DESK/PROTOCOL.

CURRENT REAL-WORLD PATTERNS

Allen & Heath documents bidirectional MIDI control over USB/TCP for current mixer
families. Current Avantis documentation includes faders, mutes, sends, DCA,
name/color, scene recall and MMC.

Yamaha documents software that remotely controls and monitors broad ranges of its
digital mixers and system devices.

Avid maintains current EUCON control-surface documentation.

AES70/OCA provides an open control/monitoring architecture designed to work
alongside media transports such as AES67, Dante and AVB.

POIETEK ADAPTER

Vendor/protocol data:
-> ConsoleAdapter
-> logical parameter path.

Example:
vendor fader NRPN
-> channel.input.17.faderDb.

DESK MOVES

Physical fader event
-> update app immediately
-> optionally record mixer automation.

APP MOVES

App fader
-> desk write
-> motorized desk moves when hardware supports it.

If desk fader is not motorized:
UI reflects requested parameter state but cannot physically move the fader.
Use pickup/ghost-position UX to prevent jumps.

END DOCUMENT 103

#################### 104_DIGITAL_AUDIO_TRANSPORT_USB_OPTICAL_NETWORK.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

104 — DIGITAL AUDIO TRANSPORT: USB, OPTICAL & NETWORK
====================================================

POIETEK ROUTING GRAPH

Console port
-> AudioEndpoint
-> channel map
-> Poietek AudioBackend.

USB AUDIO

Potential:
multichannel recording/playback
low-latency native driver path on desktop
device-specific channel count.

USB MIDI/control may be a separate logical connection.

MADI

AES10 MADI supports multichannel serial digital audio and can use fibre-optic or
coaxial point-to-point links.

A MADI bridge can provide:
many console channels
bidirectional audio
clock connection
network bridge to Dante.

ADAT OPTICAL

Useful for smaller optical multichannel expansion.
Control still usually uses a separate mechanism.

AES3

Two-channel professional digital transport.
Can participate in synchronization depending device/system design.

AES50

Point-to-point multichannel full-duplex digital audio/control-adjacent auxiliary
link. It is not generic Ethernet networking even though the physical layer uses
structured Category cable.

DANTE / AES67

Network audio.
Dante PTP clock state is monitored separately from channel subscriptions.

ANALOGUE

Balanced analogue console outputs/inputs connect through an audio interface or
network converter.
Poietek then controls/records the converter's digital side.

TRANSPORT-001 channel map stored per device profile/project.
TRANSPORT-002 reconnect validates channel identities.
TRANSPORT-003 clock lock validated before recording.
TRANSPORT-004 audio transport failure never fakes silence as valid recording.
TRANSPORT-005 optical UI identifies protocol.

END DOCUMENT 104

#################### 105_ANALOGUE_HYBRID_CONSOLE_INTEGRATION.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

105 — ANALOGUE & HYBRID CONSOLE INTEGRATION
===========================================

PURE ANALOGUE DESK

If a fader/EQ/pot has no electronic telemetry or remote automation, software
cannot know its true physical value.

Poietek must not invent it.

LEVEL A — AUDIO INTEGRATION

Console analogue I/O
-> audio interface/converter
-> Poietek.

Capabilities:
record/playback/routing
digital clock at converter
metering on digital endpoint.

No remote analogue knob control.

LEVEL B — PARTIAL AUTOMATION

Some analogue/hybrid desks expose:
motorized faders
VCA automation
mute automation
recall
digitally controlled preamps
MIDI/network control.

Poietek mirrors only those verified parameters.

LEVEL C — DIGITALLY CONTROLLED ANALOGUE

Analogue signal path with digital parameter control can expose richer two-way
state through a documented adapter.

LEVEL D — MANUAL RECALL

For non-observable controls:
store user-entered value
photo/diagram
recall notes
calibration reference.

The UI marks these MANUAL, not LIVE.

VISUAL RECALL — RESEARCH

Camera/computer-vision estimation of knob positions can assist recall, but must
remain advisory until accuracy is proven. It is not control or authoritative
telemetry.

ANALOG-001 app never moves a passive pot.
ANALOG-002 manual value visibly labelled manual.
ANALOG-003 motorized supported parameter mirrors.
ANALOG-004 converter clock can sync even when console itself is analogue.
ANALOG-005 loss of control bridge does not stop analogue audio.

END DOCUMENT 105

#################### 106_MIXER_STATE_MIRROR_AUTOMATION_CONFLICTS.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

106 — MIXER STATE MIRROR, AUTOMATION & CONFLICT RESOLUTION
==========================================================

COMPANION
packages/console/src/MixerStateMirror.ts

THE MIRROR

Keeps the latest normalized desk/app state for readable/writable parameters.

SOURCES

physical_console
poietek_ui
automation_playback
scene_recall
remote_client
system.

CONFLICT MODES

LAST TOUCH
last intentional control wins.

CONSOLE PRIORITY
physical desk wins.

POIETEK PRIORITY
app/automation wins.

AUTOMATION PRIORITY
playback automation owns controlled parameters.

PICKUP
non-motorized controls must cross software value before taking over.

READ ONLY
app displays state but writes nothing.

AUTOMATION MODES

Read
Touch
Latch
Write
Trim later.

DESK CHANGE DURING AUTOMATION RECORD

physical change
-> timestamp against transport
-> automation points
-> app UI
-> project command/automation transaction.

HIGH-RATE DATA

Meters and touch movement telemetry are not written as individual project
commands at raw device frequency.
Automation recorder downsamples/curves appropriately.

LOOP PREVENTION

Each control event has source/sequence.
Adapter must not echo Poietek-originated writes forever as new user changes.

MIRROR-001 physical fader moves UI.
MIRROR-002 app fader moves motor fader where supported.
MIRROR-003 no feedback loop.
MIRROR-004 pickup prevents parameter jump.
MIRROR-005 automation time aligns to transport.

END DOCUMENT 106

#################### 109_CONSOLE_NETWORK_SECURITY_REMOTE_CONTROL.txt ####################

POIETEK MASTER DEVELOPMENT LIBRARY — VERSION 2.4

109 — CONSOLE NETWORK SECURITY & REMOTE CONTROL
===============================================

CONTROL NETWORKS CAN MOVE REAL AUDIO PARAMETERS.

Treat them as privileged device control.

LOCAL FIRST

Default console discovery/control:
local network / direct USB.

REMOTE WAN

Use:
VPN/secure tunnel
authenticated Poietek Bridge
manufacturer-secure method.

Do not simply expose desk control TCP/UDP ports to the public internet.

Allen & Heath currently documents optional SSL for TCP MIDI on supported dLive
systems, and its remote-WAN guidance recommends protecting the network rather than
exposing a desk directly.

PERMISSIONS

console.read
console.write_mix
console.write_preamps
console.scene_recall
console.routing
console.clock_change
console.phantom_power
console.transport.

DANGEROUS ACTIONS

Clock source
sample rate
phantom power
large monitor/master gain
scene recall
routing replacement

require appropriate permission and confirmation/safety mode.

LIVE/PERFORMANCE GUARD

During active show/recording:
disable connector updates
block unsafe clock changes
rate-limit remote admin
freeze non-essential background jobs.

SEC-001 unauthenticated remote client cannot control desk.
SEC-002 read-only contributor cannot move fader.
SEC-003 WAN access defaults off.
SEC-004 control-token logs redact secrets.
SEC-005 desk remains independently operable if Poietek disconnects.

END DOCUMENT 109
